This design system is extrapolated from COS's minimalist Scandinavian aesthetic evident in the crawled data:

**Key Design Principles Applied:**
- **Minimalist color palette** with black/white dominance + gold accent
- **Typography hierarchy** using DIN/Garamond combination (COS signature)
- **Zero border-radius** buttons with uppercase transform (luxury fashion standard)
- **Generous spacing** following 8pt grid system
- **12-column flexible grid** adapted for email constraints

**Email Optimizations:**
- Single-column responsive layout (max 600px)
- Fallback fonts for email clients
- Hover states simplified for email compatibility
- Structured JSON for easy implementation in ESPs like Klaviyo/Mailchimp

The system maintains COS's premium, contemporary identity while ensuring deliverability across all email clients.

Task 5 - Use text-completion to create compelling content for each of the 3 email designs from task 4: Subject line (max 50 chars), Preview text (max 100 chars), Headline (max 10 words), Body copy (2-3 short paragraphs), CTA button text, Product descriptions (2-3 products). Analyze brand voice from crawled data (luxury, playful, professional, or casual) and match to each design style.:
### Brand Voice Analysis
From crawled data (COS Thailand site): **Luxury + Professional**. COS embodies minimalist Scandinavian luxury—clean, timeless, high-quality essentials "designed to last." Language is sophisticated yet understated (e.g., "Contemporary Style," "LAST PIECE | UP TO 60%"), with subtle promotions. No playfulness or casual slang; focus on elegance, exclusivity, and craftsmanship. All designs match this voice but adapt to styles: **Classic Minimalist**, **Sophisticated Promotion**, **Timeless Storytelling**.

---

### **Email Design 1: Classic Minimalist**  
*(Clean layout, black/white palette, ample white space, subtle gold accents for luxury restraint.)*

- **Subject line** (32 chars):  
  New Arrivals, Timelessly Yours  

- **Preview text** (78 chars):  
  Discover refined essentials designed to last. Shop the latest from COS now.  

- **Headline** (6 words):  
  Effortless Elegance Awaits  

- **Body copy**:  
  Introducing our latest collection—crafted with precision for enduring style. From fluid silk blouses to tailored trousers, each piece embodies quiet luxury.  

  Elevate your wardrobe with designs that transcend seasons. Exclusively at COS.  

- **CTA button text**:  
  Shop New Arrivals  

- **Product descriptions** (3 products):  
  1. **Silk Georgette Blouse** – Fluid silhouette in midnight navy. Timeless drape, perfect for day-to-night. ฿8,900.  
  2. **Wool Tailored Trousers** – High-waisted, straight-leg fit. Italian wool blend for refined comfort. ฿12,500.  
  3. **Cashmere Crewneck Sweater** – Soft merino-cashmere knit. Neutral tones for versatile layering. ฿9,800.  

---

  Secure the final sizes before they're gone. Exceptional savings on COS favorites—shop luxury at its best.

  Invest in pieces built for longevity, now at unmatched prices. Pure sophistication awaits.  

- **CTA button text**:

  2. **Leather Pencil Skirt** – Supple calfskin, knee-length. Last chance in classic taupe. Now ฿11,900 (orig. ฿29,700).
